  51:     public SharedAnalysisDispatcher(SharedMarketSource source, JdbcTemplate local,
  52:             CoinConfigurationService coins, Supplier<List<String>> intervals,
  53:             CandleClosedAnalysisWorker worker, Executor liveExecutor, Executor historyExecutor,
  54:             PlatformTransactionManager manager) {
  55:         this.source=source;this.coins=coins;this.intervals=intervals;this.worker=worker;
  56:         this.liveExecutor=liveExecutor;this.historyExecutor=historyExecutor;
  57:         // Independent settings: do not modify the application's shared JdbcTemplate.
  58:         jdbc=new JdbcTemplate(Objects.requireNonNull(local.getDataSource()));
  59:         jdbc.setQueryTimeout(3);
  60:         tx=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  61:         tx.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  62:         tx.setTimeout(5);
  63:     }

  65:     // FIX-140: compatibility default; production disabling is an explicit operator setting.
  66:     private volatile boolean historicalEnabled=true;
  67:     public void historicalEnabled(boolean enabled) { historicalEnabled=enabled; }
  68:     public void live() { dispatch(true); }
  69:     public void historical() { dispatch(false); }
  70:     // FIX-142: durable retry queues are not bounded by executing-thread capacity.
  71:     private volatile int historicalCapacity=1;
  72:     public void historicalCapacity(int size){if(size<1||size>4)throw new IllegalArgumentException("Historical capacity must be 1..4");historicalCapacity=size;}
  73:     private volatile boolean freshPriority;
  74:     public void freshPriority(boolean enabled){freshPriority=enabled;}
  75:     private volatile boolean acknowledgmentEnabled;
  76:     private long acknowledgmentCursor;
  77:     public void acknowledgmentEnabled(boolean enabled){acknowledgmentEnabled=enabled;}
  78:     private boolean ackRefused(String reason){Fix142Metrics.count("ACK_"+reason);return false;}
  79:     private boolean acknowledgeCompletedWork(Map<String,Object> review) {
  80:         if(!acknowledgmentEnabled||num(review,"phase")!=4||!"REVIEW_REQUIRED".equals(review.get("analysis_status")))return false;
  81:         var work=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT signal_id,symbol,interval_code,candle_open_time,status,completed_at FROM signal_processing_work WHERE source_event_id=? FOR UPDATE",review.get("source_event_id"));
  82:         Fix142Metrics.count("ACK_EXAMINED");
  83:         if(work.size()!=1) {
  84:             if(work.isEmpty()&&!jdbc.queryForList("SELECT signal_id FROM signal_processing_work WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time=? LIMIT 1",review.get("symbol"),review.get("interval_code"),review.get("candle_open_time")).isEmpty())Fix142Metrics.count("ACK_LEGACY_WORK_CANDIDATE");
  85:             return ackRefused(work.size()>1?"AMBIGUOUS_WORK_LINK":"WORK_LINK_MISSING");
  86:         }
  87:         var w=work.getFirst();
  88:         if(!"COMPLETED".equals(w.get("status"))||w.get("completed_at")==null)return ackRefused("WORK_UNRESOLVED");
  89:         for(String key:List.of("symbol","interval_code","candle_open_time"))if(!Objects.equals(review.get(key),w.get(key)))return ackRefused("WORK_IDENTITY_MISMATCH");
  90:         var conflict=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT signal_id FROM signal_processing_work WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time=? AND signal_id<>? FOR UPDATE",review.get("symbol"),review.get("interval_code"),review.get("candle_open_time"),w.get("signal_id"));
  91:         if(!conflict.isEmpty())return ackRefused("CONFLICTING_WORK");
  92:         var signals=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT id,symbol,interval_code,candle_open_time FROM trade_signal WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE",w.get("signal_id"));
  93:         if(signals.size()!=1)return ackRefused("SIGNAL_MISSING_OR_AMBIGUOUS");
  94:         for(String key:List.of("symbol","interval_code","candle_open_time"))if(!Objects.equals(review.get(key),signals.getFirst().get(key)))return ackRefused("SIGNAL_IDENTITY_MISMATCH");
  95:         int changed=jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET analysis_status='WORK_ACKNOWLEDGED',analysis_completed_at=? WHERE source_event_id=? AND phase=4 AND analysis_status='REVIEW_REQUIRED' AND (owner_token=? OR (owner_token IS NULL AND ? IS NULL))",w.get("completed_at"),review.get("source_event_id"),review.get("owner_token"),review.get("owner_token"));
  96:         if(changed!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-142 acknowledgment must affect exactly one locked row");
  97:         jdbc.update("INSERT INTO shared_delivery_resolution_audit(source_event_id,symbol,operation,old_owner_token,reviewer,evidence) VALUES(?,?,'WORK_ACKNOWLEDGED',?,'FIX-142_EXACT_CHAIN',?)",review.get("source_event_id"),review.get("symbol"),review.get("owner_token"),"COMPLETED work signal="+w.get("signal_id")+" completedAt="+w.get("completed_at"));
  98:         log.info("[FIX-142][WORK_ACKNOWLEDGED] event={}, signal={}; no execution replay",review.get("source_event_id"),w.get("signal_id"));
  99:         return true;

 101:     public void reconcileCompletedWorkAcknowledgments() {
 102:         if(stopped||!source.enabled()||!acknowledgmentEnabled)return;
 103:         List<Map<String,Object>> rows;
 104:         try{rows=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT source_event_id,symbol,interval_code FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE analysis_status='REVIEW_REQUIRED' AND source_event_id>? ORDER BY source_event_id LIMIT 64",acknowledgmentCursor);}
 105:         catch(Exception scan){log.error("[FIX-142][ACKNOWLEDGMENT_SCAN_FAILED]",scan);return;}
 106:         if(rows.isEmpty()){acknowledgmentCursor=0;return;}
 107:         for(var row:rows)try {
 108:             Boolean acknowledged=tx.execute(status->{lockSymbol(new Lane(str(row,"symbol"),str(row,"interval_code")));
 109:                 var current=jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE source_event_id=? FOR UPDATE",row.get("source_event_id"));return acknowledgeCompletedWork(current);});
 110:             Fix142Metrics.count(Boolean.TRUE.equals(acknowledged)?"ACK_RECONCILIATION_COMMITTED":"ACK_RECONCILIATION_RETAINED");
 111:         }catch(Exception failure){Fix142Metrics.count("ACK_RECONCILIATION_FAILED");log.error("[FIX-142][ACKNOWLEDGMENT_FAILED] event={}",row.get("source_event_id"),failure);}
 112:         finally{acknowledgmentCursor=num(row,"source_event_id");}
 113:     }
 114:     public void stop() { stopped=true; }

 116:     private List<Lane> lanes() {
 117:         var result=new ArrayList<Lane>();
 118:         // Preserve discovery of the existing standard delivery intervals even if
 119:         // a legacy Binance configuration lists fewer locally subscribed periods.
 120:         var periods=new LinkedHashSet<>(List.of("1m","5m","15m","1h","4h","1d"));
 121:         periods.addAll(intervals.get());
 122:         for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols())
 123:             for(String interval:periods)result.add(new Lane(symbol,interval));
 124:         return result;
 125:     }
 126: 
 127:     private void dispatch(boolean live) {
 128:         if(stopped || !source.enabled() || (!live && !historicalEnabled))return;
 129:         long started=System.nanoTime();
 130:         Set<Lane> poolActive=live?liveActive:historyActive;
 131:         int capacity=live?8:historicalCapacity;
 132:         if(poolActive.size()>=capacity)return;
 133:         int checked=0, submitted=0;
 134:         try {
 135:             var lanes=lanes();
 136:             // Bounded round-robin turns. A blocked lane cannot monopolize selection.
 137:             for(int n=0;n<Math.min(16,lanes.size()) && !stopped;n++) {
 138:                 if(poolActive.size()>=capacity)break;
 139:                 int cursor=live?liveCursor++:historyCursor++;
 140:                 Lane lane=lanes.get(Math.floorMod(cursor,lanes.size()));
 141:                 checked++;
 142:                 if(!admissionGate.test(lane.symbol()) || !active.add(lane))continue;
 143:                 poolActive.add(lane);
 144:                 boolean handedOff=false;
 145:                 try {
 146:                     var candidate=candidate(lane,live,Instant.now());
 147:                     if(candidate==null)continue;
 148:                     long id=num(candidate,"source_event_id");
 149:                     String token=UUID.randomUUID().toString();
 150:                     long claimStarted=System.nanoTime();
 151:                     boolean claimed=Boolean.TRUE.equals(tx.execute(status->claim(lane,candidate,live,token)));
 152:                     long claimMs=(System.nanoTime()-claimStarted)/1_000_000;
 153:                     if(!claimed)continue;
 154:                     long queued=System.nanoTime();
 155:                     try {
 156:                         (live?liveExecutor:historyExecutor).execute(()->run(lane,candidate,live,token,queued));
 157:                         handedOff=true;submitted++;Fix142Metrics.count(live?"LIVE_SUBMITTED":"HISTORY_SUBMITTED");
 158:                         log.info("[FIX-138][CLAIM] mode={}, event={}, lane={}, closeUtc={}, claimMs={}",
 159:                             live?"LIVE":"HISTORY",id,lane,time(candidate,"candle_close_time"),claimMs);
 160:                     } catch(RejectedExecutionException rejected) {
 161:                         // AbortPolicy guarantees the task did not start. Roll back ONLY
 162:                         // this known unsubmitted claim; uncertain execute failures go to review.
 163:                         tx.executeWithoutResult(s->{lockSymbol(lane);
 164:                             jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET analysis_status=?,owner_token=NULL,analysis_started_at=NULL WHERE source_event_id=? AND owner_token=? AND analysis_status='RUNNING'",
 165:                                 str(candidate,"analysis_status"),id,token);
 166:                         });
 167:                         log.warn("[FIX-138][POOL_FULL] mode={}, event={}, lane={}; unsubmitted claim released",live?"LIVE":"HISTORY",id,lane);
 168:                     } catch(RuntimeException uncertain) {
 169:                         log.error("[FIX-138][SUBMISSION_UNCONFIRMED] event={}, lane={}; durable owner retained",id,lane,uncertain);
 170:                     }
 171:                 } catch(Exception failure) {
 172:                     log.error("[FIX-138][LANE_DISPATCH_FAILED] mode={}, lane={}",live?"LIVE":"HISTORY",lane,failure);
 173:                 } finally {
 174:                     if(!handedOff) {poolActive.remove(lane);active.remove(lane);}
 175:                 }
 176:             }
 177:         } catch(Exception failure) { log.error("[FIX-138][DISPATCH_FAILED] mode={}",live?"LIVE":"HISTORY",failure); }
 178:         finally {
 179:             long elapsed=(System.nanoTime()-started)/1_000_000;
 180:             long previous=live?liveHeartbeat:historyHeartbeat;
 181:             if(started-previous>=TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(30) || elapsed>=5000) {
 182:                 if(live)liveHeartbeat=started;else historyHeartbeat=started;

 184:                     live?"LIVE":"HISTORY",checked,submitted,poolActive.size(),elapsed,source.poolPressure());
 185:             }
 186:         }

 189:     private Map<String,Object> candidate(Lane lane,boolean live,Instant now) {
 190:         long started=System.nanoTime();
 191:         Map<String,Object> result=null;
 192:         Instant cutoff=now.minusSeconds(SharedEventPolicy.closeGraceSeconds(lane.interval()));
 193:         // FIX-140: attributed retry work is selected independently of candle grace,
 194:         // so an expired retry can be terminalized instead of remaining stuck forever.
 195:         if(live) {
 196:             var retry=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=?  197:             if(!retry.isEmpty())return retry.getFirst();
 198:         }
 199:         // Single-status indexed ranges avoid IN-list sorting across the entire backlog.
 200:         for(String status:live?List.of("PENDING"):List.of("PENDING","HISTORICAL_DEFERRED")) {
 201:             String window=live
 202:                 ? " AND status='COMPLETED' AND candle_close_time>=? AND candle_close_time<=?"
 203:                 : " AND candle_close_time<?";
 204:             var args=new ArrayList<Object>(List.of(lane.symbol(),lane.interval(),status,Timestamp.from(cutoff)));
 205:             if(live)args.add(Timestamp.from(now));
 206:             args.add(Timestamp.from(now));
 207:             var rows=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND phase=4 AND analysis_status=? AND closed=1"
 208:                 +window+" AND (analysis_not_before IS NULL OR analysis_not_before<?) ORDER BY candle_close_time"+(live&&freshPriority?" DESC,symbol_sequence DESC":" ASC,symbol_sequence ASC")+" LIMIT 1",args.toArray());
 209:             if(!rows.isEmpty() && (result==null || before(rows.getFirst(),result)))result=rows.getFirst();
 210:         }
 211:         long ms=(System.nanoTime()-started)/1_000_000;
 212:         if(ms>=1000)log.warn("[FIX-138][SLOW_CANDIDATE] mode={}, lane={}, elapsedMs={}",live?"LIVE":"HISTORY",lane,ms);
 213:         return result;
 214:     }
 215:     private boolean before(Map<String,Object> a,Map<String,Object> b) {
 216:         int order=time(a,"candle_close_time").compareTo(time(b,"candle_close_time"));
 217:         return order<0 || (order==0 && num(a,"symbol_sequence")<num(b,"symbol_sequence"));
 218:     }
 219:     private Map<String,Object> lockSymbol(Lane lane) {
 220:         return jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=? FOR UPDATE",lane.symbol());
 221:     }

 223:     private boolean claim(Lane lane,Map<String,Object> selected,boolean live,String token) {
 224:         if(stopped || !admissionGate.test(lane.symbol()) || (!live && !historicalEnabled) || !SharedCutoverPolicy.approved(lockSymbol(lane)))return false;
 225:         long id=num(selected,"source_event_id");
 226:         var e=jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE source_event_id=? FOR UPDATE",id);
 227:         Instant now=Instant.now();
 228:         if(num(e,"phase")!=4 || !truth(e.get("closed"))
 229:                 || !Set.of("PENDING","HISTORICAL_DEFERRED","EXECUTION_RETRY").contains(str(e,"analysis_status")))return false;
 230:         if(time(e,"analysis_not_before")!=null && !time(e,"analysis_not_before").isBefore(now))return false;
 231:         boolean fresh=SharedEventPolicy.closeEligible(lane.interval(),time(e,"candle_close_time"),now);
 232:         boolean retry="EXECUTION_RETRY".equals(str(e,"analysis_status"));
 233:         if(retry && !live)return false;
 234:         if(live && ((!fresh && !retry) || !"COMPLETED".equals(str(e,"status"))))return false;
 235:         if(!live && !time(e,"candle_close_time").isBefore(now.minusSeconds(SharedEventPolicy.closeGraceSeconds(lane.interval()))))return false;
 236: 
 237:         // Both clocks/JVMs acquire symbol -> delivery locks in the same order.
 238:         // Every RUNNING owner blocks, including an owner later than this old candle.
 239:         var running=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT source_event_id FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE analysis_status='RUNNING' AND symbol=? AND interval_code=? LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE",lane.symbol(),lane.interval());
 240:         if(!running.isEmpty()) {blocked(lane,"RUNNING_OWNER",num(running.getFirst(),"source_event_id"));return false;}
 241:         // Bound reconciliation too. A completed analysis-only failure can be isolated,
 242:         // but never re-executed automatically. Any work or wallet evidence retains review.
 243:         var reviews=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE analysis_status='REVIEW_REQUIRED' AND symbol=? AND interval_code=? ORDER BY symbol_sequence LIMIT 17 FOR UPDATE",lane.symbol(),lane.interval());
 244:         if(reviews.size()>16) {blocked(lane,"REVIEW_BATCH_LIMIT",num(reviews.getFirst(),"source_event_id"));return false;}
 245:         for(var review:reviews) {
 246:             if(!isolateAnalysisOnlyReview(review) && !acknowledgeCompletedWork(review)) {blocked(lane,"UNRESOLVED_EXECUTION_REVIEW",num(review,"source_event_id"));return false;}
 247:         }
 248:         // A historical task may not claim a lane with ready fresh work. It does
 249:         // not become live if the clock changes; false authority is passed explicitly.
 250:         if(!live && candidate(lane,true,now)!=null)return false;
 251:         return jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET analysis_status='RUNNING',owner_token=?,analysis_started_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),analysis_completed_at=NULL WHERE source_event_id=? AND analysis_status IN ('PENDING','HISTORICAL_DEFERRED','EXECUTION_RETRY')",token,id)==1;
 252:     }
 253: 
 254:     private boolean isolateAnalysisOnlyReview(Map<String,Object> review) {
 255:         long id=num(review,"source_event_id");
 256:         // Completion marker is mandatory. Age alone NEVER proves a stopped worker.
 257:         if(review.get("analysis_completed_at")==null || num(review,"phase")!=4)return false;
 258:         if(!hasNoDurableEvidence(review))return false;
 259:         jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET analysis_status='ANALYSIS_ONLY_REVIEW' WHERE source_event_id=? AND analysis_status='REVIEW_REQUIRED'",id);
 260:         // Original error, ownership, timestamps and signal remain intact. This is
 261:         // an isolated analysis failure, NOT a claim that analysis succeeded.
 262:         log.info("[FIX-138][ANALYSIS_REVIEW_ISOLATED] event={}; completed attempt, no processing work or wallet evidence; no retry; transaction pending commit",id);
 263:         return true;
 264:     }
 265: 
 266:     /** FIX-141: the durable no-evidence check shared by isolateAnalysisOnlyReview (completed
 267:      * attempts) and reconcileOwnerTimeoutReviews (owner-timeout attempts, see below). Checks
 268:      * signal_processing_work and wallet_trade only - never phase/completion, which each caller
 269:      * gates differently. Caller must hold the row's lock (FOR UPDATE on the review) already. */
 270:     private boolean hasNoDurableEvidence(Map<String,Object> review) {
 271:         long id=num(review,"source_event_id");
 272:         var workByEvent=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT signal_id FROM signal_processing_work WHERE source_event_id=? LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE",id);
 273:         var workByCandle=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT signal_id FROM signal_processing_work WHERE symbol=? AND candle_open_time=? AND interval_code=? LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE",
 274:             review.get("symbol"),review.get("candle_open_time"),review.get("interval_code"));
 275:         if(!workByEvent.isEmpty() || !workByCandle.isEmpty())return false;
 276:         var signals=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT id FROM trade_signal WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time=? FOR UPDATE",
 277:             review.get("symbol"),review.get("interval_code"),review.get("candle_open_time"));
 278:         for(var signal:signals) {
 279:             if(!jdbc.queryForList("SELECT signal_id FROM signal_processing_work WHERE signal_id=? FOR UPDATE",signal.get("id")).isEmpty())return false;
 280:             if(!jdbc.queryForList("SELECT id FROM wallet_trade WHERE signal_id=? LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE",signal.get("id")).isEmpty())return false;
 281:         }
 282:         return true;
 283:     }
 284: 
 285:     // FIX-141 Phase 2: claim()'s REVIEW_BATCH_LIMIT check (reviews.size()>16, above) runs BEFORE
 286:     // any row is ever examined, so a lane that crosses that count has no path back down - nothing
 287:     // ever calls isolateAnalysisOnlyReview on those rows to shrink it. This pass runs independently
 288:     // of claim(), globally (not per-lane-capped), so the backlog actually drains over successive
 289:     // scheduled invocations instead of being permanently gated by the same limit it needs to clear.
 290:     //
 291:     // Review correction: locking is now symbol -> delivery, matching claim()'s established
 292:     // order (previously this locked the delivery row first, then the symbol - reversed from
 293:     // every existing lock acquisition, a deadlock risk against claim()). The outer scan is
 294:     // unlocked and reads only the identifying columns needed to take the symbol lock; the
 295:     // delivery row is re-read and revalidated under both locks before anything is isolated.
 296:     //
 297:     // Review correction: the scan also now advances a cursor by source_event_id instead of
 298:     // always starting at symbol_sequence 0. A fixed "ORDER BY ... LIMIT 64" re-selects the
 299:     // same leading rows every run, so any row that fails isolation (real evidence found)
 300:     // permanently blocks every later-ordered eligible row from ever being examined. The
 301:     // cursor advances past every row this pass looked at - cleared or not - and wraps back
 302:     // to the start once it reaches the end, so the whole backlog gets fair rotation instead
 303:     // of stalling behind whatever sits first.
 304:     private volatile long reconcileAnalysisOnlyCursor=0;
 305: 
 306:     public void reconcileAnalysisOnlyReviews() {
 307:         if(stopped || !source.enabled())return;
 308:         long cursor=reconcileAnalysisOnlyCursor;
 309:         List<Map<String,Object>> rows;
 310:         try {
 311:             rows=jdbc.queryForList(
 312:                 "SELECT source_event_id,symbol,interval_code FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE analysis_status='REVIEW_REQUIRED' AND analysis_completed_at IS NOT NULL AND source_event_id>? ORDER BY source_event_id LIMIT 64",cursor);
 313:             if(rows.isEmpty() && cursor>0) {
 314:                 // Nothing left ahead of the cursor: wrap around so rows before it (whose
 315:                 // evidence may have changed since they were last looked at) get revisited.
 316:                 cursor=0;
 317:                 rows=jdbc.queryForList(
 318:                     "SELECT source_event_id,symbol,interval_code FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE analysis_status='REVIEW_REQUIRED' AND analysis_completed_at IS NOT NULL AND source_event_id>? ORDER BY source_event_id LIMIT 64",cursor);
 319:             }
 320:         } catch(Exception failure) {
 321:             log.error("[FIX-141][REVIEW_BACKLOG_RECONCILIATION_FAILED] scan query failed; cursor unchanged",failure);
 322:             return;
 323:         }
 324:         // Review correction: the cursor previously advanced only after the WHOLE batch
 325:         // succeeded, inside a single try/catch around the entire loop. One row throwing
 326:         // (e.g. a lock-wait timeout) escaped the loop, skipped the cursor update entirely, and
 327:         // left every row behind it - including the thrown-on row itself - stuck ahead of an
 328:         // unmoved cursor, to be re-attempted (and potentially re-fail) on every future pass.
 329:         // The cursor now advances past each row as it is attempted, success or failure, inside
 330:         // a per-row try/catch, so a single bad row is skipped (retried only after the next
 331:         // wrap-around) instead of stalling everything behind it.
 332:         int isolated=0;
 333:         long lastSeen=cursor;
 334:         for(var row:rows) {
 335:             long id=num(row,"source_event_id");
 336:             Fix142Metrics.count("ISOLATION_EXAMINED");
 337:             try {
 338:                 Boolean cleared=tx.execute(status->{
 339:                     // Symbol lock first, then the delivery row - claim()'s order. Everything
 340:                     // about the row is revalidated once both locks are held.
 341:                     lockSymbol(new Lane(str(row,"symbol"),str(row,"interval_code")));
 342:                     var current=jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE source_event_id=? FOR UPDATE",id);
 343:                     if(!"REVIEW_REQUIRED".equals(str(current,"analysis_status")))return false;
 344:                     return isolateAnalysisOnlyReview(current);
 345:                 });
 346:                 if(Boolean.TRUE.equals(cleared)){isolated++;Fix142Metrics.count("ISOLATION_COMMITTED");}
 347:                 else Fix142Metrics.count("ISOLATION_RETAINED");
 348:             } catch(Exception failure) {
 349:                 Fix142Metrics.count("ISOLATION_FAILED");
 350:                 log.warn("[FIX-141][REVIEW_RECONCILE_ROW_FAILED] event={}; left REVIEW_REQUIRED, will be retried after the cursor wraps",id,failure);
 351:             } finally {
 352:                 lastSeen=id;
 353:                 reconcileAnalysisOnlyCursor=lastSeen;
 354:             }
 355:         }
 356:         if(rows.size()<64)reconcileAnalysisOnlyCursor=0;
 357:         if(isolated>0)log.info("[FIX-141][REVIEW_BACKLOG_RECONCILED] isolated={}, checked={}",isolated,rows.size());
 358:     }
 359: 
 360:     // FIX-141 Phase 2 review correction: this method NO LONGER MUTATES STATE. Quiet time plus
 361:     // the absence of durable evidence is not owner fencing - this codebase has no cross-JVM
 362:     // process-liveness or heartbeat mechanism, so an old worker that merely stalled (GC pause,
 363:     // slow network, suspended container) could still be alive and resume after this pass looked
 364:     // at the row. Being disabled by default was not a sufficient safeguard, since enabling it
 365:     // would still let a resumed old worker race a row this pass had already isolated. The
 366:     // mutation (the UPDATE to ANALYSIS_ONLY_REVIEW) is removed entirely until real fencing
 367:     // (a durable liveness/heartbeat check) and transaction-resolution evidence exist.
 368:     //
 369:     // What remains is diagnostic-only: it locks and re-reads each candidate the same way the
 370:     // old mutating path did, checks hasNoDurableEvidence(...), and logs a candidate for an
 371:     // operator to inspect and act on manually. It performs no write to analysis_status or
 372:     // last_error. The enable flag now only controls whether this diagnostic logging runs.
 373:     //
 374:     // Review correction: locking is symbol -> delivery (claim()'s order), not delivery -> symbol
 375:     // as before. The scan also advances a source_event_id cursor instead of a fixed
 376:     // "ORDER BY analysis_started_at LIMIT 16", so a row that keeps failing the no-evidence check
 377:     // cannot permanently block later-ordered rows from ever being looked at.
 378:     private volatile boolean ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled=false;
 379:     public void ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled(boolean enabled) { ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled=enabled; }
 380:     private volatile long ownerTimeoutQuietSeconds=3600;
 381:     public void ownerTimeoutQuietSeconds(long seconds) { ownerTimeoutQuietSeconds=seconds; }
 382:     private volatile long ownerTimeoutReviewCursor=0;
 383: 
 384:     public void reconcileOwnerTimeoutReviews() {
 385:         if(stopped || !source.enabled() || !ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled)return;
 386:         long cursor=ownerTimeoutReviewCursor;
 387:         List<Map<String,Object>> rows;
 388:         try {
 389:             var cutoff=Timestamp.from(Instant.now().minusSeconds(ownerTimeoutQuietSeconds));
 390:             rows=jdbc.queryForList(
 391:                 "SELECT source_event_id,symbol,interval_code FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE analysis_status='REVIEW_REQUIRED' "+
 392:                 "AND analysis_completed_at IS NULL AND phase=4 AND analysis_started_at<? AND source_event_id>? ORDER BY source_event_id LIMIT 16",cutoff,cursor);
 393:             if(rows.isEmpty() && cursor>0) {
 394:                 cursor=0;
 395:                 rows=jdbc.queryForList(
 396:                     "SELECT source_event_id,symbol,interval_code FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE analysis_status='REVIEW_REQUIRED' "+
 397:                     "AND analysis_completed_at IS NULL AND phase=4 AND analysis_started_at<? AND source_event_id>? ORDER BY source_event_id LIMIT 16",cutoff,cursor);
 398:             }
 399:         } catch(Exception failure) {
 400:             log.error("[FIX-141][OWNER_TIMEOUT_RECONCILIATION_FAILED] scan query failed; cursor unchanged",failure);
 401:             return;
 402:         }
 403:         // Review correction: same per-row cursor persistence as reconcileAnalysisOnlyReviews()
 404:         // - a single row's failure must not abort the pass or skip the cursor update for the
 405:         // rows already examined before it.
 406:         int candidates=0;
 407:         long lastSeen=cursor;
 408:         for(var row:rows) {
 409:             long id=num(row,"source_event_id");
 410:             try {
 411:                 Boolean noEvidence=tx.execute(status->{
 412:                     // Symbol lock first, then the delivery row - claim()'s order. Read-only:
 413:                     // no write to analysis_status or last_error happens anywhere below.
 414:                     lockSymbol(new Lane(str(row,"symbol"),str(row,"interval_code")));
 415:                     var current=jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE source_event_id=? FOR UPDATE",id);
 416:                     if(!"REVIEW_REQUIRED".equals(str(current,"analysis_status")) || current.get("analysis_completed_at")!=null)return false;
 417:                     if(time(current,"analysis_started_at")==null || !time(current,"analysis_started_at").isBefore(Instant.now().minusSeconds(ownerTimeoutQuietSeconds)))return false;
 418:                     return hasNoDurableEvidence(current);
 419:                 });
 420:                 if(Boolean.TRUE.equals(noEvidence)) {
 421:                     candidates++;
 422:                     log.warn("[FIX-141][OWNER_TIMEOUT_CANDIDATE] event={}; quietSeconds={}, no durable evidence found; NOT isolated automatically - diagnostic only, pending real owner fencing",id,ownerTimeoutQuietSeconds);
 423:                 }
 424:             } catch(Exception failure) {
 425:                 log.warn("[FIX-141][OWNER_TIMEOUT_ROW_FAILED] event={}; skipped, will be retried after the cursor wraps",id,failure);
 426:             } finally {
 427:                 lastSeen=id;
 428:                 ownerTimeoutReviewCursor=lastSeen;
 429:             }
 430:         }
 431:         if(rows.size()<16)ownerTimeoutReviewCursor=0;
 432:         if(candidates>0)log.info("[FIX-141][OWNER_TIMEOUT_CANDIDATES] candidates={}, checked={}",candidates,rows.size());
 433:     }
 434:     private void blocked(Lane lane,String reason,long event) {
 435:         Fix142Metrics.count("CLAIM_REJECTED_"+reason);
 436:         long now=System.nanoTime();
 437:         Long previous=lastBlockedLog.get(lane);
 438:         if(previous==null || now-previous>=TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(30)) {
 439:             lastBlockedLog.put(lane,now);
 440:             log.warn("[FIX-138][LANE_BLOCKED] lane={}, reason={}, event={}",lane,reason,event);
 441:         }
 442:     }

 444:     private void run(Lane lane,Map<String,Object> e,boolean live,String token,long queued) {
 445:         long id=num(e,"source_event_id");

  20:         mode = env.getProperty("shared-market.mode", "OFF").trim();
  21:         if (!java.util.Set.of("OFF","OBSERVE","LIVE").contains(mode)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-132 invalid shared-market.mode");
  22:         this.local=local; enabled="LIVE".equals(mode);
  23:         if ("OFF".equals(mode)) { reader = local; pool = null; return; }
  24:         HikariConfig c = new HikariConfig();
  25:         c.setJdbcUrl(env.getRequiredProperty("shared-market.jdbc-url"));
  26:         c.setUsername(env.getRequiredProperty("shared-market.username"));
  27:         c.setPassword(env.getRequiredProperty("shared-market.password"));
  28:         c.setMaximumPoolSize(3); c.setMinimumIdle(0); c.setReadOnly(true);
  29:         c.setConnectionTimeout(5000); c.setValidationTimeout(2000); c.setConnectionInitSql("SET time_zone = '+00:00'");
  30:         c.setPoolName("fix132-source-read");
  31:         // Finite driver waits as well as pool/query waits; no single overall deadline is claimed.
  32:         c.addDataSourceProperty("connectTimeout", "2000");
  33:         c.addDataSourceProperty("socketTimeout", "5000");
  34:         c.addDataSourceProperty("connectionTimeZone","UTC");
  35:         c.addDataSourceProperty("forceConnectionTimeZoneToSession","true");

  37:         try {

  39:             String expectedSchema = env.getProperty("shared-market.source-schema", "crypto_ai_v2");
  40:             if(!expectedSchema.equals(reader.queryForObject("SELECT DATABASE()",String.class)))
  41:                 throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 shared connection must target configured source schema " + expectedSchema);
  42:         } catch(RuntimeException failure) { pool.close();throw failure; }
  43:     }

  23: /** FIX-131: one owner for startup/reload/health. The actor never does database or socket I/O.

  25:  * Availability is deliberately sacrificed until old work AND transport have terminated. */
  26: @Component

  29:     private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
  30:

  32:     static final long SCAN_NANOS = TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.toNanos(500);
  33:     static final long DEADLINE_NANOS = TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(15);

  36:     private final Supplier<String> urls;

  43:     private final ArrayBlockingQueue<CompletableFuture<Void>> requests = new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(32);
  44:     private final ThreadPoolExecutor connectWorker = worker("fix131-connect", 1);
  45:     private final ThreadPoolExecutor closeWorker = worker("fix131-close", 1);
  46:     private final Object lifecycle = new Object();
  47:     private final CompletableFuture<Void> stopped = new CompletableFuture<>();
  48:     private final List<CompletableFuture<Void>> waiting = new ArrayList<>();

  50:     private final java.util.Set<BinanceWebSocketHandler> cleaning = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  51:     private volatile boolean stopRequested;

  54:     private volatile Attempt current;
  55:     private long sequence;
  56:     private long interruptionStart;
  57:     private Instant revokedAt;
  58:     private long nextAttempt;
  59:     private boolean replacement;
  60:     private static final class Attempt {

  72:     }

  88:         this.connector = connector; this.gaps = gaps;
  89:     }
  90:     private static ThreadPoolExecutor worker(String name, int queue) {
  91:         return new ThreadPoolExecutor(1, 1, 0, TimeUnit.SECONDS, new ArrayBlockingQueue<>(queue), task -> {
  92:             Thread t = new Thread(task, name); t.setDaemon(true); return t;
  93:         }, new ThreadPoolExecutor.AbortPolicy());
  94:     }
  95:     @EventListener(ApplicationReadyEvent.class)
  96:     public void start() {
  97:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
  98:         synchronized (lifecycle) {
  99:             if (actor != null || stopRequested || !properties.getWebsocket().isEnabled()) return;
 100:             replacement = true;
 101:             actor = new Thread(this::run, "fix131-ws-owner");
 102:             actor.setDaemon(true);
 103:             actor.start();
 104:         }
 105:     }
 106:     void wake() { LockSupport.unpark(actor); }
 107:     private void notice(BinanceWebSocketHandler handler) {
 108:         if (handler.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.REVOKED && !handler.allClosed()) {
 109:             if (current == null || current.handler != handler) cleanup.add(handler);
 110:             // After bounded shutdown, even the unresolved CURRENT handshake retains a cleanup path.
 111:             // This fallback runs only during shutdown, never on a live processing callback.
 112:             if (closeWorker.isShutdown() && handler.gate.isDrained()) handler.close();
 113:         }
 114:         wake();
 115:     }
 116:     CompletableFuture<Void> requestReload() {
 117:         if (!properties.getWebsocket().isEnabled()) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 118:         var request = new CompletableFuture<Void>();
 119:         synchronized (lifecycle) {
 120:             if (stopRequested) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.STOPPED);
 121:             if (!requests.offer(request)) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.BUSY);
 122:         }
 123:         start(); wake();
 124:         return request;
 125:     }
 126:     public void reload() {
 127:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
 128:         var request = requestReload();
 129:         try { request.get(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS); }
 130:         catch (TimeoutException ex) { throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.WAIT_TIMEOUT); }
 131:         catch (InterruptedException ex) { Thread.currentThread().interrupt(); throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.WAIT_TIMEOUT); }
 132:         catch (ExecutionException ex) {
 133:             if (ex.getCause() instanceof RuntimeException runtime) throw runtime;
 134:             throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 135:         }
 136:     }
 137:     private WebSocketReconnectException failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason reason) {
 138:         return new WebSocketReconnectException(reason, phase);
 139:     }
 140:     private void run() {
 141:         try {
 142:             while (true) {
 143:                 // Persistent lifecycle facts are examined on EVERY pass, before more queued requests.
 144:                 tick();
 145:                 if (stopRequested && current == null && cleanup.isEmpty()) break;
 146:                 LockSupport.parkNanos(SCAN_NANOS);
 147:             }
 148:             phase = "STOPPED";
 149:             finish(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.STOPPED);
 150:             stopped.complete(null);

  53:     // FIX-124: no suspended caller transaction may retain market-data locks.
  54:     @Transactional(propagation = Propagation.NEVER)
  55:     public boolean processKline(JsonNode root) {
  56:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132: Trader kline ingestion is disabled"); }
  57:         if(drain!=null && !drain.beginInput()){log.info("[FIX-153][KLINE_REJECTED_DRAINING]");return false;}
  58:         try {
  59:

  61:                 ? root.path("data")

  63:

  66:         if (kline.isMissingNode() || kline.isNull()) {
  67:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
  68:                     "Binance message does not contain kline data"
  69:             );

  73:                 .asText()

  76: 
  77:         String intervalCode = kline.path("i")
  78:                 .asText()
  79:                 .trim();

  82:                 kline.path("t").asLong()
  83:         );
  84:

  86:                 kline.path("T").asLong()
  87:         );
  88: 
  89:         boolean closed = kline.path("x")
  90:                 .asBoolean(false);
  91:         BigDecimal livePrice = MarketPriceEventService.normalizePrice(decimal(kline, "c"));
  92: 
  93:         // FIX-153: an absent Binance event timestamp is not equivalent to a fresh receipt.
  94:         Instant observedAt = data.path("E").asLong(0L)>0 ? Instant.ofEpochMilli(data.path("E").asLong()) : null;
  95:         boolean canonical="1m".equals(intervalCode);
  96:         String token=java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
  97:         java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<CandleRepository.Transition> transition=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<>();
  98:         java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<Long> priceId=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<>();
  99:         long started=System.nanoTime();
 100:         try {
 101:             transactions.process(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,observedAt,livePrice,
 102:                 ()->{
 103:                     transition.set(candleRepository.upsertRuntime(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,closeTime,
 104:                         decimal(kline,"o"),decimal(kline,"h"),decimal(kline,"l"),livePrice,

 115:                 com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.DUPLICATE_CLOSE);

 119:                 // fail-safe: an audit problem must never make a Binance receipt fail.

### src/main/java/com/crypto/infrastructure/transaction/KlineTransactionCoordinator.java:1-130
   1: package com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction;
   2: 
   3: import org.slf4j.Logger;
   4: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   6: import org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;
   7: import org.springframework.transaction.TransactionDefinition;
   8: import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;
   9: 
  10: import java.math.BigDecimal;
  11: import java.time.Instant;
  12: 
  13: /** FIX-124: commit market input before locking a position. Execute protection
  14:  * before publishing the closed-candle event, preserving that ordering. No outer
  15:  * transaction may retain candle locks while a protection transaction runs.
  16:  */
  17: @Service
  18: public class KlineTransactionCoordinator {
  19:     private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(KlineTransactionCoordinator.class);
  20:     private static final int MAX_ATTEMPTS = 2;
  21:     private KlineTiming timing = KlineTiming.loggingOnly();
  22:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired
  23:     public void setTiming(KlineTiming timing) { this.timing = timing; }
  24:     private final TransactionTemplate transaction;
  25:     private final Fix124ProtectionStore diagnostics;
  26: 
  27:     public KlineTransactionCoordinator(PlatformTransactionManager manager, Fix124ProtectionStore diagnostics) {
  28:         transaction = new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  29:         transaction.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  30:         // FIX-153: current committed ordering after consistency locks; bound JDBC transaction work.
  31:         transaction.setIsolationLevel(TransactionDefinition.ISOLATION_READ_COMMITTED);
  32:         transaction.setTimeout(30);
  33:         this.diagnostics = diagnostics;
  34:     }
  35: 
  36:     public void process(String symbol, String interval, Instant candleOpenTime, Instant observedAt,
  37:                         BigDecimal price, Runnable persistInput, Runnable protect,
  38:                         Runnable observe, Runnable publishClosed) {
  39:         var context = timing.context(symbol, interval, candleOpenTime, observedAt, null);
  40:         // Candle and exact canonical price observation commit together. A failed
  41:         // input write must not produce an unrecorded protection decision.
  42:         try {
  43:             timing.measure(context,"INPUT_TRANSACTION",false,()->transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->persistInput.run()));
  44:             com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.INPUT_COMMITTED);
  45:         }catch(RuntimeException failure){
  46:             com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.INPUT_FAILED_OR_UNCONFIRMED);throw failure;
  47:         }
  48:         // FIX-131: executeWithoutResult returned successfully: this is input COMMIT, not callback completion.
  49:         try { com.crypto.client.binance.websocket.KlineReceipt.inputCommitted(symbol, interval, candleOpenTime, observedAt); }
  50:         catch (RuntimeException diagnosticFailure) { log.warn("[FIX-131][RECEIPT_DIAGNOSTIC_FAILED] symbol={}", symbol, diagnosticFailure); }
  51:         if (protect != null) {
  52:             int attempts = 0;
  53:             RuntimeException failure = null;
  54:             do {
  55:                 attempts++;
  56:                 try {
  57:                     timing.measure(context, "PROTECTION_TRANSACTION", false, () -> transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx -> protect.run()));
  58:                     failure = null;
  59:                     break;
  60:                 } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  61:                     failure = ex;
  62:                     // FIX-153: completion returned/failed; a commit exception alone does not prove rollback.
  63:                     // Never retry a late wallet/commit error or an unknown outcome.
  64:                     if (!(ex instanceof InitialPositionLockDeadlock) || attempts == MAX_ATTEMPTS) break;
  65:                     log.warn("[FIX-124][PRODUCTION][INITIAL_LOCK_RETRY] symbol={}, candleOpenTime={}, observedAt={}, attempt={}",
  66:                             symbol, candleOpenTime, observedAt, attempts);
  67:                 }
  68:             } while (attempts < MAX_ATTEMPTS);
  69:             if (failure != null || attempts > 1) {
  70:                 String outcome = failure == null ? "RECOVERED" : "FAILED";
  71:                 if (failure == null) {
  72:                     log.info("[FIX-124][PRODUCTION][RECOVERED] symbol={}, observedAt={}, attempts={}", symbol, observedAt, attempts);
  73:                 } else {
  74:                     com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_FAILED_OR_UNCONFIRMED);
  75:                     log.error("[FIX-124][PRODUCTION][FAILED] symbol={}, observedAt={}, attempts={}; input committed; protection completion failed or unconfirmed, consult durable price evidence",
  76:                             symbol, observedAt, attempts, failure);
  77:                 }
  78:                 diagnostics.record(symbol, interval, candleOpenTime, observedAt, price, attempts, outcome, failure);
  79:             }
  80:         }
  81:         if (observe != null) {
  82:             try {
  83:                 timing.measure(context, "OBSERVER_TRANSACTION", false, () -> transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx -> observe.run()));
  84:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  85:                 log.warn("[FIX-124][OBSERVER_FAILED] symbol={}; input committed; protection outcome remains in durable price evidence", symbol, ex);
  86:             }
  87:         }
  88:         // AFTER_COMMIT listener still receives an actual committed transaction;
  89:         // publishing outside a transaction would silently drop the analysis event.
  90:         if (publishClosed != null) timing.measure(context, "CLOSE_DISPATCH_TRANSACTION", false,
  91:                 () -> transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx -> publishClosed.run()));
  92:     }
  93: }

### src/main/java/com/crypto/indicator/event/CandleClosedEventListener.java:1-100
   1: package com.crypto.indicator.event;
   2: 
   3: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   4: import org.springframework.transaction.event.TransactionPhase;
   5: import org.springframework.transaction.event.TransactionalEventListener;
   6: 
   7: /**
   8:  * Receives the committed closed-candle event and hands it to FIX-043's ordered async dispatcher.
   9:  *
  10:  * IMPORTANT: keep this listener intentionally tiny. Before FIX-043 it performed the complete
  11:  * indicator -> signal -> wallet chain synchronously inside AFTER_COMMIT. Production evidence on
  12:  * 22 Aug 2026 showed ~97-100% candle persistence but only ~17-20% 1m indicator/signal coverage;
  13:  * the heavy listener was effectively letting the five-minute recovery scheduler become the
  14:  * analysis clock.
  15:  *
  16:  * We do NOT put @Async directly on this method because a generic async pool can reorder adjacent
  17:  * candles (for example 12:02 could start before 12:01). The dispatcher returns immediately to the
  18:  * Binance ingestion path while preserving FIFO order per symbol+interval and parallelism across
  19:  * independent streams.
  20:  */
  21: @Component
  22: public class CandleClosedEventListener {
  23: 
  24:     private final CandleAnalysisDispatcher dispatcher;
  25: 
  26:     public CandleClosedEventListener(CandleAnalysisDispatcher dispatcher) {
  27:         this.dispatcher = dispatcher;
  28:     }
  29: 
  30:     @TransactionalEventListener(phase = TransactionPhase.AFTER_COMMIT)
  31:     public void handle(CandleClosedEvent event) {
  32:         dispatcher.submit(event);
  33:     }
  34: }

  14: /** FIX-153: one running task per lane. 1m keeps latest pending; higher TFs retain
  15:  * a bounded FIFO. Reservations and admission share the lane monitor, including
  16:  * detector idle check. No analysis executes on ingestion threads at saturation. */

  22:     private final Map<String,Lane> lanes=new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
  23:     private volatile boolean stopped;
  24:     public CandleAnalysisDispatcher(@Qualifier("candleAnalysisExecutor") Executor executor,CandleClosedAnalysisWorker worker){this.executor=executor;this.worker=worker;}
  25:     public void submit(CandleClosedEvent event){admit(event,false,()->true);}
  26:     /** Check exact candle evidence under the detector's existing in-process lock.
  27:      * Check+reserve+enqueue is atomic with live admission; detector never replaces pending live work. */
  28:     public boolean submitDetected(CandleClosedEvent event,java.util.function.BooleanSupplier missing){return admit(event,true,missing);}
  29:     public boolean idle(String symbol,String interval){Lane l=lanes.get(symbol+"|"+interval);if(l==null)return !stopped;synchronized(l){return !stopped&&l.active==null&&l.pending.isEmpty();}}
  30:     /** A detector reservation is in-flight admission work, even before a task exists. */
  31:     public boolean idleAll(){return !stopped && lanes.values().stream().allMatch(l->{synchronized(l){return l.active==null&&l.pending.isEmpty()&&l.reserved.isEmpty()&&l.deferredLive.isEmpty();}});}
  32:     private boolean admit(CandleClosedEvent e,boolean detector,java.util.function.BooleanSupplier missing){
  33:         if(e==null || stopped)return false;
  34:         String key=e.symbol().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT)+"|"+e.intervalCode();
  35:         Lane lane=lanes.computeIfAbsent(key,k->new Lane());
  36:         if(detector){
  37:             synchronized(lane){
  38:                 if(stopped || lane.active!=null || !lane.pending.isEmpty() || !lane.reserved.add(e.openTime())) return false;
  39:             }
  40:             // FIX-153: reserve first, but never hold admission's monitor across database I/O.
  41:             boolean absent;
  42:             try {absent=missing.getAsBoolean();}
  43:             catch(RuntimeException failure){
  44:                 CandleClosedEvent handoff;
  45:                 synchronized(lane){
  46:                     handoff=lane.deferredLive.remove(e.openTime());
  47:                     if(handoff!=null)enqueueReserved(lane,key,handoff,false);
  48:                     else lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());
  49:                 }
  50:                 throw failure;
  51:             }
  52:             synchronized(lane){
  53:                 // A committed live close that arrived during the detector's database
  54:                 // check owns admission. Transfer it to the queue while retaining
  55:                 // its reservation and the lane monitor: no second SQL check and no
  56:                 // interleaving admission window can lose it.
  57:                 CandleClosedEvent liveHandoff=lane.deferredLive.remove(e.openTime());
  58:                 if(liveHandoff!=null)return enqueueReserved(lane,key,liveHandoff,false);
  59:                 if(!absent || stopped || lane.active!=null || !lane.pending.isEmpty()){
  60:                     lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());return false;
  61:                 }
  62:             }
  63:         }
  64:         synchronized(lane){
  65:             if(stopped || (detector && (lane.active!=null || !lane.pending.isEmpty()))){if(detector)lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());return false;}
  66:             if(!detector && lane.reserved.contains(e.openTime())){
  67:                 // Detector owns the temporary reservation only. Preserve the exact
  68:                 // committed event and let the detector hand it straight back once
  69:                 // its read completes; no live close is discarded in this window.
  70:                 lane.deferredLive.putIfAbsent(e.openTime(),e);return true;
  71:             }
  72:             if(!detector && !lane.reserved.add(e.openTime())){LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(DUPLICATE_ADMISSION);return false;}
  73:             Task task=new Task(e,detector);
  74:             if("1m".equals(e.intervalCode()) && !lane.pending.isEmpty()){
  75:                 Task previous=lane.pending.peekLast();
  76:                 // A delayed older callback cannot replace newer pending work.
  77:                 if(!e.openTime().isAfter(previous.event.openTime())){lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(OVERFLOW);return false;}
  78:                 previous=lane.pending.removeLast();lane.reserved.remove(previous.event.openTime());lane.deferredLive.remove(previous.event.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(REPLACED);
  79:                 log.info("[FIX-153][PENDING_REPLACED] lane={}, skippedOpenUtc={}, keptOpenUtc={}, businessExecuted=false",key,previous.event.openTime(),e.openTime());
  80:             }else if(lane.pending.size()>=4){lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(OVERFLOW);
  81:                 log.info("[FIX-153][LANE_OVERFLOW] lane={}, rejectedOpenUtc={}, pending={}, policy=BOUNDED_FIFO",key,e.openTime(),lane.pending.size());return false;}
  82:             lane.pending.add(task);LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(ADMITTED);
  83:             if(detector)LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(DETECTOR_ADMITTED);
  84:             return schedule(lane,key);
  85:         }
  86:     }
  87:     /** Caller holds lane's monitor and has already retained e.openTime() in reserved. */
  88:     private boolean enqueueReserved(Lane lane,String key,CandleClosedEvent e,boolean detector){
  89:         if(stopped){lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());return false;}
  90:         Task task=new Task(e,detector);
  91:         if("1m".equals(e.intervalCode()) && !lane.pending.isEmpty()){
  92:             Task previous=lane.pending.peekLast();
  93:             if(!e.openTime().isAfter(previous.event.openTime())){lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(OVERFLOW);return false;}
  94:             previous=lane.pending.removeLast();lane.reserved.remove(previous.event.openTime());lane.deferredLive.remove(previous.event.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(REPLACED);
  95:             log.info("[FIX-153][PENDING_REPLACED] lane={}, skippedOpenUtc={}, keptOpenUtc={}, businessExecuted=false",key,previous.event.openTime(),e.openTime());
  96:         }else if(lane.pending.size()>=4){lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(OVERFLOW);
  97:             log.info("[FIX-153][LANE_OVERFLOW] lane={}, rejectedOpenUtc={}, pending={}, policy=BOUNDED_FIFO",key,e.openTime(),lane.pending.size());return false;}
  98:         lane.pending.add(task);LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(ADMITTED);
  99:         if(detector)LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(DETECTOR_ADMITTED);
 100:         return schedule(lane,key);
 101:     }
 102:     private boolean schedule(Lane lane,String key){
 103:         if(stopped)return false;
 104:         if(lane.active!=null)return true;
 105:         Task task=lane.pending.pollFirst();if(task==null)return true;lane.active=task;
 106:         try {executor.execute(()->{
 107:             synchronized(lane){if(stopped || lane.active!=task)return;}
 108:             long start=System.nanoTime();LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(ANALYSIS_ATTEMPTED);LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.analysis(key,Instant.now());
 109:             String outcome="THREW_OR_UNCONFIRMED";
 110:             try {outcome=worker.processShared(task.event,task.event.closeTime(),true);}
 111:             catch(RuntimeException failure){log.error("[FIX-153][ANALYSIS_TASK_FAILED] lane={}, openUtc={}",key,task.event.openTime(),failure);}
 112:             finally {
 113:                 LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(ANALYSIS_FINISHED);
 114:                 log.debug("[FIX-153][ANALYSIS_TASK_RETURNED] lane={}, openUtc={}, outcome={}, callbackMs={}; task return alone is not wallet commit evidence",key,task.event.openTime(),outcome,(System.nanoTime()-start)/1_000_000);
 115:                 synchronized(lane){lane.reserved.remove(task.event.openTime());lane.deferredLive.remove(task.event.openTime());lane.active=null;schedule(lane,key);}
 116:             }
 117:         });return true;}catch(RejectedExecutionException failure){
 118:             // Release every queued reservation on rejection: local detector may reconsider gaps.
 119:             LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(SUBMISSION_REJECTED);lane.active=null;lane.reserved.remove(task.event.openTime());
 120:             lane.deferredLive.remove(task.event.openTime());
 121:             for(Task queued:lane.pending){lane.reserved.remove(queued.event.openTime());lane.deferredLive.remove(queued.event.openTime());}lane.pending.clear();
 122:             log.info("[FIX-153][EXECUTOR_REJECTED] lane={}, openUtc={}, ranOnIngestionThread=false",key,task.event.openTime());
 123:             return false;
 124:         }
 125:     }
 126:     public Map<String,Object> snapshot(){Map<String,Object> result=new TreeMap<>();lanes.forEach((k,l)->{synchronized(l){result.put(k,Map.of("active",l.active!=null,"pending",l.pending.size(),"reservations",l.reserved.size()));}});return result;}
 127:     @PreDestroy public void stop(){stopped=true;lanes.values().forEach(l->{synchronized(l){l.pending.clear();l.reserved.clear();l.deferredLive.clear();}});}

  17: @Service
  18: public class MarketDataService {
  19:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
  20:     private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
  21: 
  22: 
  23:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient client;
  24:     private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
  25:     private final TransactionTemplate transaction;
  26: 
  27:     public MarketDataService(BinanceMarketDataClient client,CandleRepository candleRepository,PlatformTransactionManager manager){
  28:         this.client=client;this.candleRepository=candleRepository;this.transaction=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  29:     }
  30: 
  31:     public int importCandles(String symbol, String interval, int limit) {
  32:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132: collector owns candle imports"); }
  33:         String normalizedSymbol = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  34:         // Do not trust a potentially skewed local clock to finalise Binance's
  35:         // currently-open REST candle. Read it before klines: a later timestamp
  36:         // could otherwise turn an in-flight response row into a false close.
  37:         java.time.Instant exchangeNow=client.getServerTime();
  38:         List<BinanceKline> klines = client.getKlines(normalizedSymbol, interval, limit);
  39:         // No database transaction is open while Binance is contacted. The local
  40:         // writes remain one transaction once the bounded network calls return.
  41:         Integer upserted=transaction.execute(status->{
  42:             int count=0;
  43:             for (BinanceKline kline : klines) {
  44:                 // FIX-153: an in-progress REST candle is not a close event or historical evidence.
  45:                 if (kline.closeTime().isAfter(exchangeNow)) continue;
  46:                 count += candleRepository.upsert(
  47:                         normalizedSymbol,interval,kline.openTime(),kline.closeTime(),
  48:                         kline.openPrice(),kline.highPrice(),kline.lowPrice(),kline.closePrice(),
  49:                         kline.volume(),kline.quoteAssetVolume(),kline.numberOfTrades(),
  50:                         kline.takerBuyBaseVolume(),kline.takerBuyQuoteVolume(),true);
  51:             }
  52:             return count;
  53:         });
  54:         if(upserted==null)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 local candle import transaction returned no result");
  55:         return upserted;
  56:     }
  57: 
  58:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
  59:     public List<Candle> latestCandles(String symbol, String interval) {
  60:         return candleRepository
  61:                 .findTop500BySymbolAndIntervalCodeOrderByOpenTimeDesc(
  62:                         symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT), interval)
  63:                 .stream()
  64:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(Candle::getOpenTime))
  65:                 .toList();
  66:     }
  67: 
  68:     private Candle toEntity(String symbol, String interval, BinanceKline kline) {
  69:         return Candle.builder()
  70:                 .symbol(symbol)
  71:                 .intervalCode(interval)
  72:                 .openTime(kline.openTime())
  73:                 .closeTime(kline.closeTime())
  74:                 .openPrice(kline.openPrice())
  75:                 .highPrice(kline.highPrice())
  76:                 .lowPrice(kline.lowPrice())
  77:                 .closePrice(kline.closePrice())
  78:                 .volume(kline.volume())
  79:                 .quoteAssetVolume(kline.quoteAssetVolume())
  80:                 .numberOfTrades(kline.numberOfTrades())
  81:                 .takerBuyBaseVolume(kline.takerBuyBaseVolume())
  82:                 .takerBuyQuoteVolume(kline.takerBuyQuoteVolume())
  83:                 .closed(true)
  84:                 .build();
  85:     }
  86: }

  46:     @Value("${analysis.scoring.data-quality.minimum-candles:210}") private int minimumCandles;
  47:     @Value("${local-candle.reconciliation.live-close-grace-seconds:10}") private long liveCloseGraceSeconds;
  48:     @Value("${local-candle.reconciliation.safety-margin-seconds:2}") private long safetyMarginSeconds;
  49: 
  50:     public LocalCandleReconciliationService(JdbcTemplate jdbc, CoinConfigurationService coins,

  59:     public void scheduledScan(){ request("SCHEDULE"); }
  60:     public void requestAfterReconnect(long generation){

  63:     }
  64:     public boolean isIdle(){return !running.get();}
  65:     public Map<String,Object> health(){
  66:         Map<String,Object> result=new LinkedHashMap<>();result.put("enabled",enabled);result.put("inFlight",running.get());
  67:         try {
  68:             result.put("pendingCandidates",jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM local_reconciliation_audit WHERE status='PENDING'",Long.class));
  69:             result.put("recoveredCount",jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM local_reconciliation_audit WHERE status='RECOVERED'",Long.class));
  70:             result.put("gapBackfilledCount",jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM local_reconciliation_audit WHERE status='GAP_BACKFILLED'",Long.class));
  71:             result.put("expiredCount",jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM local_reconciliation_audit WHERE status='EXPIRED'",Long.class));
  72:             Instant local=Instant.now();Instant reference=exchangeClock==null?local:exchangeClock.correctJvm(local);
  73:             List<Map<String,Object>> activeGaps=new ArrayList<>();
  74:             for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols())for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()){
  75:                 Instant lower=reference.minusSeconds(Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*intervalSeconds(interval));
  76:                 Timestamp first=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT MIN(open_time) FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=?",Timestamp.class,symbol,interval);
  77:                 if(first==null)continue;
  78:                 Long count=jdbc.queryForObject("""
  79:                     SELECT COUNT(*) FROM local_reconciliation_audit
  80:                     WHERE subject_type='CANDLE_DATA' AND symbol=? AND interval_code=?
  81:                       AND status='EXPIRED' AND failure_reason='NOT_ON_EXCHANGE'
  82:                       AND candle_open_time>=? AND candle_open_time>?
  83:                     """,Long.class,symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(lower),first);
  84:                 if(count!=null&&count>0)activeGaps.add(Map.of("symbol",symbol,"interval",interval,"count",count));
  85:             }
  86:             result.put("exchangeGapBlockedByLane",activeGaps);
  87:             result.put("exchangeGapBlockedCount",activeGaps.stream().mapToLong(row->((Number)row.get("count")).longValue()).sum());
  88:         }catch(RuntimeException unavailable){result.put("status","NOT_MIGRATED_OR_UNAVAILABLE");}
  89:         return result;
  90:     }
  91: 
  92:     private void request(String reason){
  93:         if(!enabled || !"OFF".equals(mode) || !running.compareAndSet(false,true))return;
  94:         try { executor.execute(()-> { try { runPass(reason); } finally { running.set(false); } }); }
  95:         catch(RuntimeException rejected){running.set(false);log.warn("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_REJECTED] reason={}",reason,rejected);}
  96:     }
  97: 
  98:     private void runPass(String reason){
  99:         if(drain!=null&&!drain.beginReconciliation())return;
 100:         try {
 101:             // The server-time read happens before every REST kline request and is
 102:             // the sole exchange-time reference for this pass.
 103:             Instant exchangeNow=binance.getServerTime();
 104:             Instant databaseNow=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)",Timestamp.class).toInstant();
 105:             int[] remaining={Math.max(0,requestBudget)};
 106:             for(Candidate candidate:openCandidates(exchangeNow,databaseNow)) {
 107:                 if(drain!=null&&drain.draining())return;
 108:                 if(remaining[0]--<=0)break;
 109:                 recover(candidate,exchangeNow,databaseNow,false);
 110:             }
 111:             if(remaining[0]>0) for(Candidate candidate:gapCandidates(exchangeNow,databaseNow)) {
 112:                 if(drain!=null&&drain.draining())return;
 113:                 if(remaining[0]--<=0)break;
 114:                 recover(candidate,exchangeNow,databaseNow,true);
 115:             }
 116:             log.debug("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_PASS] reason={}, exchangeNow={}, budget={}",reason,exchangeNow,requestBudget-remaining[0]);
 117:         } catch(RuntimeException failure) {
 118:             log.warn("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_PASS_FAILED] reason={}; no candle was treated as recovered",reason,failure);
 119:         } finally {if(drain!=null)drain.completeReconciliation();}
 120:     }
 121: 
 122:     private List<Candidate> openCandidates(Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow){
 123:         List<Candidate> result=new ArrayList<>();
 124:         for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()) {
 125:             long seconds=intervalSeconds(interval), window=Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*seconds;
 126:             Instant lower=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(window);
 127:             Instant eligible=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(liveCloseGraceSeconds+safetyMarginSeconds);
 128:             Instant latestOpen=eligible.minusSeconds(seconds);
 129:             result.addAll(jdbc.query("""
 130:                 SELECT symbol,interval_code,open_time FROM candle
 131:                 WHERE closed=0 AND interval_code=? AND open_time>=? AND open_time<=?
 132:                   AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL')
 133:                 ORDER BY open_time ASC LIMIT 50
 134:                 """,(rs,n)->{
 135:                     Instant open=rs.getTimestamp(3).toInstant();
 136:                     return new Candidate(rs.getString(1),rs.getString(2),open,expectedClose(open,rs.getString(2)));
 137:                 },interval,Timestamp.from(lower),Timestamp.from(latestOpen)));
 138:         }
 139:         return result.stream().filter(c->audit.due(c.symbol,c.interval,c.openTime,databaseNow))
 140:             .filter(this::positiveLossEvidence).toList();
 141:     }
 142: 
 143:     /** Read-only discovery of absent expected identities in the active quality window. */
 144:     private List<Candidate> gapCandidates(Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow){
 145:         List<Candidate> result=new ArrayList<>();
 146:         for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols()) for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()) {
 147:             long seconds=intervalSeconds(interval), window=Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*seconds;
 148:             Instant eligible=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(liveCloseGraceSeconds+safetyMarginSeconds);
 149:             Instant first=Instant.ofEpochSecond((exchangeNow.minusSeconds(window).getEpochSecond()/seconds)*seconds);
 150:             Set<Instant> present=new HashSet<>(jdbc.query("""
 151:                 SELECT open_time FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time>=? AND open_time<?
 152:                 """,(rs,n)->rs.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(first),Timestamp.from(eligible)));
 153:             for(Instant open=first;open.plusSeconds(seconds).compareTo(eligible)<=0;open=open.plusSeconds(seconds)) {
 154:                 if(!present.contains(open) && audit.due(symbol,interval,open,databaseNow))
 155:                     result.add(new Candidate(symbol,interval,open,open.plusSeconds(seconds).minusMillis(1)));
 156:             }
 157:         }
 158:         return result;
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     private void recover(Candidate candidate,Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow,boolean gap) {
 162:         var prior=audit.findCandle(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime);
 163:         int attempts=prior.map(s->s.attempts()+1).orElse(1);
 164:         try {
 165:             List<BinanceKline> rows=binance.getKlines(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,candidate.closeTime);
 166:             List<BinanceKline> exact=rows.stream().filter(k->k.openTime().equals(candidate.openTime)&&k.closeTime().equals(candidate.closeTime)).toList();
 167:             if(rows.isEmpty() && gap) {
 168:                 List<BinanceKline> first=binance.getKlinesAtOrAfter(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime);
 169:                 if(!first.isEmpty() && first.getFirst().openTime().isAfter(candidate.openTime)) {
 170:                     terminalUnavailable(candidate,exchangeNow,"NOT_ON_EXCHANGE");return;
 171:                 }
 172:                 pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_EMPTY",exchangeNow);return;
 173:             }
 174:             if(rows.isEmpty()) { pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_EMPTY",exchangeNow);return; }
 175:             if(candidate.closeTime.compareTo(exchangeNow)>=0) {
 176:                 pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_NOT_FINAL",exchangeNow);return;
 177:             }
 178:             if(exact.size()!=1) {
 179:                 pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_MISMATCH",exchangeNow);return;
 180:             }
 181:             BinanceKline k=exact.getFirst();
 182:             tx.executeWithoutResult(status->{
 183:                 boolean changed=gap ? candles.insertAuthoritativeGapBackfill(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,k.closeTime(),
 184:                     k.openPrice(),k.highPrice(),k.lowPrice(),k.closePrice(),k.volume(),k.quoteAssetVolume(),k.numberOfTrades(),k.takerBuyBaseVolume(),k.takerBuyQuoteVolume())
 185:                     : candles.finalizeExistingOpenFromAuthoritative(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,k.closeTime(),
 186:                     k.openPrice(),k.highPrice(),k.lowPrice(),k.closePrice(),k.volume(),k.quoteAssetVolume(),k.numberOfTrades(),k.takerBuyBaseVolume(),k.takerBuyQuoteVolume());
 187:                 String result=changed ? (gap?"GAP_BACKFILLED":"RECOVERED") : (gap?"ALREADY_PRESENT":"ALREADY_CLOSED");
 188:                 audit.terminal(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,result,null,exchangeNow,k.openTime(),k.closeTime());
 189:                 if(changed) outcomes.record(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE",result,null,gap?"AUTHORITATIVE_GAP_BACKFILL":"AUTHORITATIVE_RECOVERY");
 190:             });
 191:         } catch(RuntimeException failure) { pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_FAILURE",exchangeNow); }
 192:     }
 193: 
 194:     private void pending(Candidate c,int attempts,Instant databaseNow,String reason,Instant exchangeNow){
 195:         long delay=Math.min(300,attempts==1?5:attempts==2?15:60L*(1L<<Math.min(2,attempts-3)));
 196:         audit.pending(c.symbol,c.interval,c.openTime,attempts,databaseNow.plusSeconds(delay),reason,exchangeNow);
 197:     }
 198:     private boolean positiveLossEvidence(Candidate candidate){
 199:         // This relies on the current FIX-131 invariant: one primary WebSocket
 200:         // session invokes processKline synchronously and in Binance receive
 201:         // order. If streams are sharded or processing becomes asynchronous,
 202:         // this successor proof must be redesigned and re-tested.
 203:         Instant successor=candidate.openTime.plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(candidate.interval));
 204:         Integer successorPresent=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=?",Integer.class,
 205:             candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,Timestamp.from(successor));
 206:         return successorPresent!=null&&successorPresent>0 || (lastReconnectAdmission!=null && !lastReconnectAdmission.isBefore(candidate.closeTime));
 207:     }
 208:     private void terminalUnavailable(Candidate candidate,Instant exchangeNow,String reason){
 209:         tx.executeWithoutResult(status->{
 210:             audit.terminal(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"EXPIRED",reason,exchangeNow,null,null);
 211:             outcomes.record(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"SKIPPED_DATA_UNAVAILABLE",reason,null,"AUTHORITATIVE_RECONCILIATION");
 212:         });
 213:     }
 214:     private static long intervalSeconds(String interval){return switch(interval){case "1m"->60;case "5m"->300;case "15m"->900;case "1h"->3600;case "4h"->14400;case "1d"->86400;default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported interval: "+interval);};}
 215:     private static Instant expectedClose(Instant open,String interval){return open.plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval)).minusMillis(1);}
 216:     private record Candidate(String symbol,String interval,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime) {}
 217: }

   1: package com.crypto.local;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.administration.service.CoinConfigurationService;
   4: import com.crypto.indicator.event.*;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
   7: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
   8: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   9: import org.slf4j.*;
  10: import java.time.*;
  11: import java.sql.Timestamp;
